- **health-advisor**: The owner's health advisor agent. Use when the owner calls it (by role or by the persona name they gave it) or asks about their health rather than their fitness: a lab result or blood test, a check-up, a screening, a vaccine, a symptom, a medication, a visit to book, their prevention plan. Transcribes lab reports into data/body/labs/, keeps a plan with dates, explains in plain words; never diagnoses, and sends urgent symptoms straight to the emergency number. Not a doctor. Training and food are the fitness-coach's. ([danivellagit/personal-knowledge-graph](https://github.com/danivellagit/personal-knowledge-graph/tree/HEAD/skills/health-advisor) · ★1)
- **health-advisor-case-state**: Профиль пациента как ОБЯЗАТЕЛЬНЫЙ вход, реестр проверенных фактов и журнал открытых вопросов. Текущее значение аналита не хранится, а ВЫЧИСЛЯЕТСЯ свёрткой на явную дату; вывод принимает значение только как расписку, выданную в этом же вызове; факт с истёкшим TTL отдаётся с явным «устарело», а не молча; открытый блокирующий вопрос делает вывод в своей области непостроимым. ([djd1m/dz-harness](https://github.com/djd1m/dz-harness/tree/HEAD/packages/health-advisor/skills/case-state) · ★1)
- **health-advisor-clinical-decision-support**: Generate professional clinical decision support (CDS) documents for pharmaceutical and clinical research settings, including patient cohort analyses (biomarker-stratified with outcomes) and treatment recommendation reports (evidence-based guidelines with decision algorithms). Supports GRADE evidence grading, statistical analysis (hazard ratios, survival curves, waterfall plots), biomarker integration, and regulatory compliance. Outputs publication-ready LaTeX/PDF format optimized for drug development, clinical research, and evidence synthesis. ([djd1m/dz-harness](https://github.com/djd1m/dz-harness/tree/HEAD/packages/health-advisor/skills/clinical-decision-support) · ★1)
- **health-advisor-clinical-diagnostic-reasoning**: Identify and counteract cognitive biases in medical decision-making through systematic error analysis and contextual algorithm application. For diagnostic reasoning, treatment decisions, and clinical judgment improvement. NOT for basic medical knowledge, technical procedures, or non-clinical healthcare domains. ([djd1m/dz-harness](https://github.com/djd1m/dz-harness/tree/HEAD/packages/health-advisor/skills/clinical-diagnostic-reasoning) · ★1)
- **health-advisor-clinical-nlp-extractor**: Extracts medical entities (Diseases, Medications, Procedures) from unstructured clinical text using regex and simple rules (or LLM wrappers). ([djd1m/dz-harness](https://github.com/djd1m/dz-harness/tree/HEAD/packages/health-advisor/skills/clinical-nlp-extractor) · ★1)
- **health-advisor-clinical-targets**: Клиническая ЦЕЛЬ отдельно от лабораторного РЕФЕРЕНСА. Показывает оба числа рядом, считает расхождение «в референсе, но выше цели», печатает цитату источника порога и рамку «это не диагноз». Порог применяется только при совпавшем контексте пациента; при неизвестном контексте так и говорит. ([djd1m/dz-harness](https://github.com/djd1m/dz-harness/tree/HEAD/packages/health-advisor/skills/clinical-targets) · ★1)
- **health-advisor-clinicaltrials-database**: Query ClinicalTrials.gov via API v2. Search trials by condition, drug, location, status, or phase. Retrieve trial details by NCT ID, export data, for clinical research and patient matching. ([djd1m/dz-harness](https://github.com/djd1m/dz-harness/tree/HEAD/packages/health-advisor/skills/clinicaltrials-database) · ★1)
- **health-advisor-clinpgx**: Query the ClinPGx REST API for pharmacogenomic data. Look up gene-drug interactions, clinical annotations, CPIC guidelines, and FDA drug labels with pharmacogenomic information. ([djd1m/dz-harness](https://github.com/djd1m/dz-harness/tree/HEAD/packages/health-advisor/skills/clinpgx) · ★1)
- **health-advisor-critical-appraisal**: Deterministic transparency checks on a published study or registered trial — retraction status, retracted citations, registration timing, registry record changes, enrollment actuality, results timeliness. Reports dated record facts with refutation paths; never a score, never an accusation of intent. ([djd1m/dz-harness](https://github.com/djd1m/dz-harness/tree/HEAD/packages/health-advisor/skills/critical-appraisal) · ★1)
- **health-advisor-deep-research**: Execute autonomous multi-step deep research on any topic. Use when the user asks for comprehensive research, literature reviews, competitive analysis, topic deep-dives, or wants to understand a complex subject from multiple angles. Triggers on "deep research", "research on", "investigate", "literature review", "comprehensive analysis", "what do we know about", "summarize research on". ([djd1m/dz-harness](https://github.com/djd1m/dz-harness/tree/HEAD/packages/health-advisor/skills/deep-research) · ★1)
- **health-advisor-drug-interaction-checker**: Checks for potential drug-drug interactions (DDIs) between a list of medications. ([djd1m/dz-harness](https://github.com/djd1m/dz-harness/tree/HEAD/packages/health-advisor/skills/drug-interaction-checker) · ★1)
- **health-advisor-emergency-card**: Генерация карточек экстренной медицинской информации для быстрого доступа в чрезвычайных ситуациях. Используйте, когда пользователю нужна подготовка к путешествию, визиту к врачу, экстренной ситуации, или когда он спрашивает про "экстренную информацию", "медицинскую карточку", "карту первой помощи". Извлекает ключевую информацию (аллергии, лекарства, острые состояния, имплантаты), поддерживает многоформатный вывод (JSON, текст, QR-код) для экстренной помощи или быстрого обращения к врачу. ([djd1m/dz-harness](https://github.com/djd1m/dz-harness/tree/HEAD/packages/health-advisor/skills/emergency-card) · ★1)
- **health-advisor-fitness-analyzer**: Анализ данных о физической активности, выявление паттернов тренировок, оценка прогресса в фитнесе и предоставление персонализированных тренировочных рекомендаций. Поддерживает корреляционный анализ с данными о хронических заболеваниях. ([djd1m/dz-harness](https://github.com/djd1m/dz-harness/tree/HEAD/packages/health-advisor/skills/fitness-analyzer) · ★1)
- **health-advisor-health-trend-analyzer**: Анализ трендов и паттернов данных о здоровье за определённый период. Корреляция изменений лекарств, симптомов, жизненных показателей, результатов анализов и других индикаторов здоровья. Выявление тревожных тенденций, улучшений и предоставление аналитических выводов на основе данных. Используется, когда пользователь спрашивает о трендах здоровья, паттернах, изменениях с течением времени или «Что изменилось в моём здоровье?». Поддерживает многомерный анализ (вес/ИМТ, симптомы, приверженность приёму лекарств, результаты анализов, настроение и сон), корреляционный анализ, обнаружение изменений и… ([djd1m/dz-harness](https://github.com/djd1m/dz-harness/tree/HEAD/packages/health-advisor/skills/health-trend-analyzer) · ★1)
- **health-advisor-lab-results**: Lab Results agent for healthcare workflows. ([djd1m/dz-harness](https://github.com/djd1m/dz-harness/tree/HEAD/packages/health-advisor/skills/lab-results) · ★1)
- **health-advisor-medical-entity-extractor**: Extract medical entities (symptoms, medications, lab values, diagnoses) from patient messages. ([djd1m/dz-harness](https://github.com/djd1m/dz-harness/tree/HEAD/packages/health-advisor/skills/medical-entity-extractor) · ★1)
- **health-advisor-multi-search-engine**: Multi search engine integration with 17 engines (8 CN + 9 Global). Supports advanced search operators, time filters, site search, privacy engines, and WolframAlpha knowledge queries. No API keys required. ([djd1m/dz-harness](https://github.com/djd1m/dz-harness/tree/HEAD/packages/health-advisor/skills/multi-search-engine) · ★1)
- **health-advisor-nutrition-analyzer**: Анализ данных о питании, выявление пищевых паттернов, оценка нутритивного статуса и предоставление персонализированных рекомендаций по питанию. Поддерживает корреляционный анализ с данными о физической активности, сне и хронических заболеваниях. ([djd1m/dz-harness](https://github.com/djd1m/dz-harness/tree/HEAD/packages/health-advisor/skills/nutrition-analyzer) · ★1)
- **health-advisor-patiently-ai**: Patiently AI simplifies medical documents for patients. Takes doctor's letters, test results, prescriptions, discharge summaries, and clinical notes and explains them in clear, personalised language. Built by PharmaTools.AI. ([djd1m/dz-harness](https://github.com/djd1m/dz-harness/tree/HEAD/packages/health-advisor/skills/patiently-ai) · ★1)
- **health-advisor-preanalytical-guard**: Pre-analytical guard — checks what distorts a lab value BEFORE the value is interpreted. Encodes, per analyte, which sampling conditions confound it (with an effect magnitude and a source) and which companion analytes it cannot be read without. Use before any lab interpretation. ([djd1m/dz-harness](https://github.com/djd1m/dz-harness/tree/HEAD/packages/health-advisor/skills/preanalytical-guard) · ★1)
- **health-advisor-pubmed-search**: Search PubMed for scientific literature. Use when the user asks to find papers, search literature, look up research, find publications, or asks about recent studies. Triggers on "pubmed", "papers", "literature", "publications", "research on", "studies about". ([djd1m/dz-harness](https://github.com/djd1m/dz-harness/tree/HEAD/packages/health-advisor/skills/pubmed-search) · ★1)
- **health-advisor-rehabilitation-analyzer**: Анализ данных реабилитационных тренировок, выявление паттернов восстановления, оценка прогресса реабилитации и предоставление персонализированных рекомендаций. ([djd1m/dz-harness](https://github.com/djd1m/dz-harness/tree/HEAD/packages/health-advisor/skills/rehabilitation-analyzer) · ★1)
- **health-advisor-sleep-analyzer**: Анализ данных сна, выявление паттернов сна, оценка качества сна и предоставление персонализированных рекомендаций по улучшению. Поддержка корреляционного анализа с другими данными о здоровье. ([djd1m/dz-harness](https://github.com/djd1m/dz-harness/tree/HEAD/packages/health-advisor/skills/sleep-analyzer) · ★1)
- **health-advisor-tooluniverse-drug-drug-interaction**: Comprehensive drug-drug interaction (DDI) prediction and risk assessment. Analyzes interaction mechanisms (CYP450, transporters, pharmacodynamic), severity classification, clinical evidence grading, and provides management strategies. Supports single drug pairs, polypharmacy analysis (3+ drugs), and alternative drug recommendations. Use when users ask about drug interactions, medication safety, polypharmacy risks, or need DDI assessment for clinical decision support. ([djd1m/dz-harness](https://github.com/djd1m/dz-harness/tree/HEAD/packages/health-advisor/skills/tooluniverse-drug-drug-interaction) · ★1)
- **health-advisor-tooluniverse-drug-research**: Generates comprehensive drug research reports with compound disambiguation, evidence grading, and mandatory completeness sections. Covers identity, chemistry, pharmacology, targets, clinical trials, safety, pharmacogenomics, and ADMET properties. Use when users ask about drugs, medications, therapeutics, or need drug profiling, safety assessment, or clinical development research.
